Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The geology variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 576 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 420 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_576(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 576. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^420(S, F) is trivial. QED.
